{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES

  PROPERTIES
  {
    CaptionML=[ENU=ADV Avis Payment Cheque;
               FRA=Paiement cheque];
    OnInitReport=BEGIN
                   MessageDebug("Gen. Journal Line".GETFILTERS);
                 END;

    OnPreReport=VAR
                  Session_L@1000000000 : Record 2000000009;
                BEGIN

                  TransfertNo := "Gen. Journal Line".GETFILTER("Document No.");

                  IF TransfertNo = '' THEN
                   ERROR(Text000);

                  CompanyInformation.GET;
                  FormatAddress.Company(CompanyAddr,CompanyInformation);
                  GLSetup.GET;

                  //--> 20140819-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-1

                  IF CompanyInformation.Name = 'TERNOVEO' THEN BEGIN
                    text005 :=  STRSUBSTNO(Text0055,'St Quentin') ;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    text005 :=  STRSUBSTNO(Text0055,'St Laurent') ;
                  END;
                  //<-- 20140819-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-1
                  Session_L.SETRANGE(Session_L."Ma session",TRUE);
                  IF  Session_L.FINDFIRST  THEN;
                  IF Session_L."Nom de la base de donn'es"='NAV-ADVITAM-PRODUCTION' THEN BEGIN
                    CLEAR(NomBddTest_G);
                    ArchRep_G := 'NavAgri';
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    NomBddTest_G :=  COPYSTR('TEST : ' + Session_L."Nom de la base de donn'es",1, MAXSTRLEN( NomBddTest_G));
                    ArchRep_G := 'TEST';
                  END;
                   Month_G := DATE2DMY(TODAY,2);
                    Year_G := DATE2DMY(TODAY,3);
                  //-->20141008-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-1
                  IF Tttrecap_G THEN BEGIN
                    Recap_G := 'R';
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    Recap_G := 'I';
                  END;
                  TypImprime_G := 'PAICHEQUE';
                  VersionMapping_G := '00011';
                  //<--20141008-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-1

                  CLEAR(CampMapping_G);
                  TableCampagne_G.SETFILTER(TableCampagne_G."Starting Date", '<= %1', TODAY);
                  TableCampagne_G.SETFILTER(TableCampagne_G."Ending Date", '>= %1', TODAY);
                  IF TableCampagne_G.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                    IF NOT EVALUATE(Camp1_G, TableCampagne_G."No.") THEN BEGIN
                      Camp1_G :=0;
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                       CampMapp_G :=( Camp1_G * 10000) + ( Camp1_G + 1);
                    END;
                  END;
                END;

    OnPostReport=BEGIN
                   BankAccountBuffer.DELETEALL;
                 END;

  }

                                       Tablelecrituretemp_G."Remaining Amount" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Remaining Amount" ;
                                       Tablelecrituretemp_G."Applies-to ID" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Applies-to ID";
                                       Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document Date" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Document Date";
                                       Tablelecrituretemp_G."External Document No." := TableCustledgerentry_L."External Document No.";
                                       Tablelecrituretemp_G."Due Date" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Due Date";
                                       Tablelecrituretemp_G.Letter := TableCustledgerentry_L.Letter;
                                       IF NOT Tablelecrituretemp_G.INSERT THEN BEGIN
                                       END;
                                       // Appel fonction pour ecrit lettree param( n› , code lettre) pour Customer
                                       IF TableCustledgerentry_L.Letter <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                         ClientEcritureLettre(TableCustledgerentry_L."Customer No.", TableCustledgerentry_L.Letter);
                           VendorEcritureLettre(TableCustledgerentry_L."Customer No.", TableCustledgerentry_L.Letter);
                                         // Appel fonction pour lettrage compensation (n› seq , Cust/vendor)
                                         "C/VEcritureLettre"( TableCustledgerentry_L."Customer No.",
                                            TableCustledgerentry_L."Entry No.",Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor"::Customer);
                                       END;

                           //           DraftCounting := DraftCounting + 1;

                           ////////////////////////////////            CurrReport.SKIP;
                                     END;

                                 END;
                               END;

    PROCEDURE ClientEcritureLettre@1000000020(Client_P@1000000000 : Code[20];Lettre_P@1000000001 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TableCustledgerentry_L@1000000002 : Record 21;
    BEGIN
            TableCustledgerentry_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.", Client_P);
            TableCustledgerentry_L.SETRANGE(Letter,Lettre_P);
            TableCustledgerentry_L.SETRANGE("Same G/L Acc. Set Off", FALSE);
            IF TableCustledgerentry_L.FINDSET() THEN BEGIN
              REPEAT
                TableCustledgerentry_L.CALCFIELDS("Original Amount");
                Tablelecrituretemp_G.SETRANGE("Customer / Vendor", Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor"::Customer);
                Tablelecrituretemp_G.SETRANGE(Tablelecrituretemp_G."Entry No.", TableCustledgerentry_L."Entry No.");
                IF Tablelecrituretemp_G.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor" := Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor"::Customer;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Entry No." := TableCustledgerentry_L."Entry No.";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Cust./Vend. No." := TableCustledgerentry_L."Customer No.";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Posting Date" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Posting Date";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document Type" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Document Type" ;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document No." := TableCustledgerentry_L."Document No.";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G.Description := TableCustledgerentry_L.Description;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Currency Code" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Currency Code";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G.Amount := TableCustledgerentry_L.Amount;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Original Amount" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Original Amount";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Remaining Amount" :=TableCustledgerentry_L."Remaining Amount" ;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Applies-to ID" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Applies-to ID";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document Date" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Document Date";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."External Document No." := TableCustledgerentry_L."External Document No.";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Due Date" := TableCustledgerentry_L."Due Date";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G.Letter := TableCustledgerentry_L.Letter;
                  IF NOT Tablelecrituretemp_G.INSERT THEN BEGIN
                  END;
                  "C/VEcritureLettre"( TableCustledgerentry_L."Customer No.",
                     Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor"::Customer, TableCustledgerentry_L."Entry No.");

                END ELSE BEGIN
                END;
              UNTIL  TableCustledgerentry_L.NEXT =0;
            END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE VendorEcritureLettre@1000000000(Client_P@1000000001 : Code[20];Lettre_P@1000000000 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TableVendorLedgerEntry_L@1000000002 : Record 25;
    BEGIN
            TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("Vendor No.", Client_P);
            TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE(Letter,Lettre_P);
            TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("Same G/L Acc. Set Off", FALSE);
            IF TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.FINDSET() THEN BEGIN
              REPEAT
              TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.CALCFIELDS("Original Amount");
                Tablelecrituretemp_G.SETRANGE("Customer / Vendor", Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor"::Vendor);
                Tablelecrituretemp_G.SETRANGE(Tablelecrituretemp_G."Entry No.", TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Entry No.");
                IF Tablelecrituretemp_G.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor" := Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor"::Vendor;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Entry No." := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Entry No.";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Cust./Vend. No." := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Vendor No.";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Posting Date" := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Posting Date";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document Type" := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Document Type" ;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document No." := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Document No.";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G.Description := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.Description;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Currency Code" := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Currency Code";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G.Amount := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.Amount;
                   Tablelecrituretemp_G."Original Amount":= TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Original Amount";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Remaining Amount" := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Remaining Amount" ;
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Applies-to ID" := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Applies-to ID";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document Date" := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Document Date";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."External Document No." := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."External Document No.";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G."Due Date" := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Due Date";
                  Tablelecrituretemp_G.Letter := TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.Letter;
                  IF NOT Tablelecrituretemp_G.INSERT THEN BEGIN
                  END;
                  // Appel fonction pour lettrage compensation (n› seq , Cust/vendor)
                  "C/VEcritureLettre"(TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Vendor No.",
                     Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor"::Vendor, TableVendorLedgerEntry_L."Entry No.");

                END ELSE BEGIN
                END;
              UNTIL  TableVendorLedgerEntry_L.NEXT =0;
            END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "C/VEcritureLettre"@1000000001(Client_P@1000000001 : Code[20];"Cust/Vend_P"@1000000003 : 'Customer,Vendor';"Entry No_P"@1000000000 : Integer);
    VAR
      TableCVLedgerEntry_L@1000000002 : Record 8053380;
      Wletter_L@1000000004 : Code[10];
      booTrouve_L@1000000005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      IF TableCVLedgerEntry_L.GET("Cust/Vend_P", "Entry No_P") THEN BEGIN
        IF TableCVLedgerEntry_L."CV Entry Letter" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          Wletter_L := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."CV Entry Letter";
      //    TableCVLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE(TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Customer / Vendor","Cust/Vend_P");
          TableCVLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE( "Cust./Vend. No." ,Client_P);
          TableCVLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE(TableCVLedgerEntry_L."CV Entry Letter",Wletter_L);
          TableCVLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("Same G/L Acc. Set Off", FALSE);
          IF TableCVLedgerEntry_L.FINDSET() THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              TableCVLedgerEntry_L.CALCFIELDS("Original Amount");
              Tablelecrituretemp_G.SETRANGE("Customer / Vendor", TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Customer / Vendor");
              Tablelecrituretemp_G.SETRANGE(Tablelecrituretemp_G."Entry No.", TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Entry No.");
              IF Tablelecrituretemp_G.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Customer / Vendor" := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Customer / Vendor";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Entry No." := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Entry No.";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Cust./Vend. No." := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Cust./Vend. No.";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Posting Date" := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Posting Date";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document Type" := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Document Type" ;
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document No." := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Document No.";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G.Description := TableCVLedgerEntry_L.Description;
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Currency Code" := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Currency Code";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G.Amount := TableCVLedgerEntry_L.Amount;
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Original Amount" := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Original Amount";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Remaining Amount" :=TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Remaining Amount" ;
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Applies-to ID" := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Applies-to ID";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Document Date" := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Document Date";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."External Document No." := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."External Document No.";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G."Due Date" := TableCVLedgerEntry_L."Due Date";
                Tablelecrituretemp_G.Letter := TableCVLedgerEntry_L.Letter;
                IF NOT Tablelecrituretemp_G.INSERT THEN BEGIN
                END;
              END ELSE BEGIN
              END;
            UNTIL  TableCVLedgerEntry_L.NEXT =0;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

  <Code>Shared Solde as Decimal
Public Function SoldeProgDebitBlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
  Solde = Solde + Value
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function SoldeProgCrebitBlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
  Solde = Solde - Value
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function SoldeProg()
    Return Solde
End Function

Public Function SoldeProgRaz()
   Solde = 0
End Function


Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function
REM Reset Page Number:

Shared offset as Integer
Shared newPage as Object
Shared currentgroup1 as Object
Shared currentgroup2 as Object
Shared currentgroup3 as Object

Public Function GetGroupPageNumber(NewPage as Boolean, pagenumber as Integer) as Object
  If NewPage
    offset = pagenumber - 1
  End If
  Return pagenumber - offset
End Function

Public Function IsNewPage(group1 as Object, group2 as Object, group3 as Object) As Boolean
newPage = FALSE
If Not (group1 = currentgroup1)
    newPage = TRUE
    currentgroup1 = group1
    currentgroup2 = group2
    currentgroup3 = group3
     ELSE 
      If Not (group2 = currentgroup2)
        newPage = TRUE 
        currentgroup2 = group2
        currentgroup3 = group3
        ELSE
          If Not (group3 = currentgroup3)
          newPage = TRUE 
          currentgroup3 = group3
         End If
     End If
  End If
Return newPage
End Function

Shared Data1 as Object
Shared Data2 as Object
Shared Data3 as Object
Shared Data4 as Object
Shared Data5 as Object

Public Function GetData(Num as Integer, Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data1),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 2 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data2),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data3),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 4 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data4),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 5 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data5),Chr(177))))
End If

End Function

Public Function SetData(NewData as Object,Group as integer)
  If Group = 1 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data1 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 2 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data2 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 3 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data3 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 4 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data4 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 5 and NewData &lt;&gt; "" Then
      Data5 = NewData
  End If

End Function

OBJECT [Report 50061 VisPrix ]
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=24/08/22;
    Heure=09:33:31;
    Modifi'=Oui;
    Liste versions=ADV01.01.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    CaptionML=[ENU=Vis - Prix;
               FRA=Vis - Prix];
    OnPreReport=BEGIN
                  CompanyInfo.GET;
                  MessageDebugActif_G := TRUE;
                END;

  }
  DATAITEMS
  {
    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemTable=Table111;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        SETRANGE("Sales Shipment Line"."Posting Date", debDate_G, EndDate_G);

                        SETFILTER(Type, FORMAT(Type::Item) + '|' + FORMAT(Type::"Charge (Item)"));
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           RPDDate@1000000000 : Date;
                         BEGIN
                           CLEAR(TabSaison_G);
                           IF Quantity = "Quantity Invoiced" THEN BEGIN
                             CurrReport.SKIP;
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             TableSalesOrderGrooups_G.INIT;
                             IF TableSalesShipmentHeader_G.GET("Document No.") THEN BEGIN
                           //    IF TableCustomer_G.GET(TableSalesShipmentHeader_G."Bill-to Customer No.") THEN BEGIN
                           //      IF TableContact_G.GET(TableSalesShipmentHeader_G."Bill-to Customer No.") THEN BEGIN
                               IF TableCustomer_G.GET(TableSalesShipmentHeader_G."Sell-to Customer No.") THEN BEGIN
                                 IF TableContact_G.GET(TableSalesShipmentHeader_G."Sell-to Customer No.") THEN BEGIN

                                 END ELSE BEGIN
                                   CLEAR(TableContact_G);
                                 END;
                                 IF TableCustomerTiersRgpt_G.GET(TableCustomer_G."Third Grouping") THEN BEGIN
                                 END ELSE BEGIN
                                   CLEAR(TableCustomerTiersRgpt_G);
                                 END;

                               END;
                              TableContactSaleperson_G.SETRANGE("Contact Code",TableSalesShipmentHeader_G."Sell-to Customer No.");
                              TableContactSaleperson_G.SETFILTER( "Prod. Activity Code",'PV');
                              TableContactSaleperson_G.SETFILTER( TableContactSaleperson_G."Sales Network Code",'APPRO');
                              TableContactSaleperson_G.SETFILTER( TableContactSaleperson_G."Debit Responsible",'=%1',TRUE);
                              IF  TableContactSaleperson_G.FINDFIRST() THEN BEGIN
                               TableContactSaleperson_G.CALCFIELDS(TableContactSaleperson_G.Name);
                              END ELSE BEGIN
                                CLEAR(TableContactSaleperson_G);
                              END;

                             END;


                             CLEAR(Res_PolTaxMtt_G);
                             TableSalesHeader_G.SETRANGE(TableSalesHeader_G."No.","Order No.");
                             IF NOT TableSalesHeader_G.FINDFIRST() THEN BEGIN
                               CLEAR(TableSalesHeader_G);
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               IF NOT TableSalesLine_G.GET(TableSalesHeader_G."Document Type", "Order No.", "Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                                 CLEAR(TableSalesLine_G);
                               END ELSE BEGIN

                           // mettre TC DE la Fiche T8053160 code activite deproduction " PV "  et Code Reseau commercial Appro , Code Vendeur

                           // ne prendre que celui Coch' "Resp Debit"
                           // puis acces   TableSalespersonPurchaser_G pour nom sur code de table 8053160

                           //      IF NOT  TableSalespersonPurchaser_G.GET(TableSalesLine_G."Salesperson Code") THEN BEGIN
                           //        CLEAR(TableSalespersonPurchaser_G);
                               END;
                               CLEAR(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G);
                               IF TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G.GET(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Document Type"::Order,TableSalesLine_G."Document No.",
                                 TableSalesLine_G."Line No.",'REX')   THEN BEGIN
                                // WREMREX_G  :='REX';
                                 WREMREX_G:=TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Discount Amount";
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 WREMREX_G := 0;
                               END;

                               CLEAR(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G);
                               IF TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G.GET(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Document Type"::Order,TableSalesLine_G."Document No.",
                                 TableSalesLine_G."Line No.",'AVG')   THEN BEGIN
                              //   WREMAVG_G  :='AVG';
                                    WREMAVG_G  :=TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Discount Amount";
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 WREMAVG_G := 0;
                               END;
                               CLEAR(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G);
                               IF TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G.GET(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Document Type"::Order,TableSalesLine_G."Document No.",
                                 TableSalesLine_G."Line No.",'RFC')   THEN BEGIN
                               //  WREMRFC_G  :='RFC';
                               WREMRFC_G:=TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Discount Amount";
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 WREMRFC_G := 0;
                               END;

                           //    IF NOT TableSeason_G.GET(TableSalesLine_G."Season ID No.")  THEN BEGIN
                           //      CLEAR(TableSeason_G);
                           //    END;
                               IF TableSalesOrderGrooups_G.GET(TableSalesHeader_G."SO Group Code") THEN;

                             END;
                             // Calcul Rpd
                             // <-- 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
                             RPDDate := DateFact_G;
                             GetPostingDateFromSalesShipt("Sales Shipment Line", RPDDate);
                             // --> 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
                             IF TableItem_G.GET("Sales Shipment Line"."No.") THEN BEGIN
                               // <-- 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
                               //Res_PolTaxMtt_G := TableItem_G.CalcUnitPollutionTaxe(DateFact_G, "Sales Shipment Line"."Location Code",
                               //  "Sales Shipment Line"."Variant Code");
                               Res_PolTaxMtt_G := TableItem_G.CalcUnitPollutionTaxe(RPDDate, "Sales Shipment Line"."Location Code",
                                 "Sales Shipment Line"."Variant Code");
                               // --> 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
                             END;
                             // Calcul CVO
                             CLEAR( "CVO Credit Tax Code_P");
                           //  IF TableItem_G."CVO Corn" THEN BEGIN
                            //   "CVO Credit Tax Code_P":='CVO_MAIS';
                             //END;
                             IF Type = Type::"Charge (Item)"  THEN BEGIN
                                 UnitPriceCVO_G :=0;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               IF TableItem_G."CVO Credit" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                                 "CVO Credit Tax Code_P" :='CVO BLE';
                                 UnitPriceCVO_G := TableSalesLine_G.CalcCVO(TableSalesLine_G."CMC Campaign No.","CVO Credit Tax Code_P",TableItem_G) ;
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 UnitPriceCVO_G :=0;
                               END;

                             END;
                             // Ligne Commentaire Plusieurs Possibles
                             CLEAR(WtextComment_G);
                             TableSalesCommentLine_G.SETRANGE("Document Type",TableSalesCommentLine_G."Document Type"::Order);
                             TableSalesCommentLine_G.SETRANGE("No.",TableSalesLine_G."Document No.");
                             TableSalesCommentLine_G.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",TableSalesLine_G."Line No.");
                              TableSalesCommentLine_G.SETRANGE(Type,TableSalesCommentLine_G.Type::Commercial);
                             IF TableSalesCommentLine_G.FINDSET() THEN BEGIN
                               REPEAT
                                WtextComment_G :=    WtextComment_G  + TableSalesCommentLine_G.Comment + ' ' ;
                               UNTIL TableSalesCommentLine_G.NEXT = 0;
                             END;

                           // --> 20150818-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-KRE-002
                           //CLEAR(IdSaison_G);
                           //CLEAR(NomSaison_G);
                           TableSalesLine_G.SETRANGE(TableSalesLine_G."Document No.","Order No." );

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemTable=Table6661;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        SETRANGE("Posting Date", debDate_G, EndDate_G);

                        SETFILTER(Type, FORMAT(Type::Item) + '|' + FORMAT(Type::"Charge (Item)"));
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           RPDDate@1000000000 : Date;
                         BEGIN
                           CLEAR(TabSaison_G);
                           IF Quantity = "Quantity Invoiced" THEN BEGIN
                             CurrReport.SKIP;
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             TableSalesOrderGrooups_G.INIT;
                             CLEAR(Res_PolTaxMtt_G);
                               CLEAR(TableReturnReceiptHeader_G);
                             IF  TableReturnReceiptHeader_G.GET("Document No.") THEN BEGIN
                               IF NOT TableCustomer_G.GET(TableReturnReceiptHeader_G."Sell-to Customer No.") THEN BEGIN
                                 CLEAR(TableCustomer_G);
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 IF TableContact_G.GET(TableReturnReceiptHeader_G."Sell-to Customer No.") THEN BEGIN
                                 END ELSE BEGIN
                                   CLEAR(TableContact_G);
                                 END;
                                 IF TableCustomerTiersRgpt_G.GET(TableCustomer_G."Third Grouping") THEN BEGIN
                                 END ELSE BEGIN
                                   CLEAR(TableCustomerTiersRgpt_G);
                                 END;
                               END;
                               TableContactSaleperson_G.SETRANGE("Contact Code",TableReturnReceiptHeader_G."Sell-to Customer No.");
                               TableContactSaleperson_G.SETRANGE( "Prod. Activity Code",'PV');
                               TableContactSaleperson_G.SETRANGE( TableContactSaleperson_G."Sales Network Code",'APPRO');
                               TableContactSaleperson_G.SETFILTER( TableContactSaleperson_G."Debit Responsible",'=%1',TRUE);
                               IF  TableContactSaleperson_G.FINDFIRST() THEN BEGIN
                               TableContactSaleperson_G.CALCFIELDS(TableContactSaleperson_G.Name);
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 CLEAR(TableContactSaleperson_G) ;
                               END;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                                CLEAR(TableReturnReceiptHeader_G);
                             END;
                           //  IF NOT  TableSalespersonPurchaser_G.GET(TableReturnReceiptHeader_G."Salesperson Code") THEN BEGIN
                           //    CLEAR(TableSalespersonPurchaser_G);
                           //  END;
                             // Acces T 36 pour Retour Ventes
                             IF TableSalesLineretour_G.GET(TableSalesLineretour_G."Document Type"::"Return Order",
                               "Return Order No.","Return Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                               IF TableSalesHeaderRetour_G.GET(TableSalesLineretour_G."Document Type"::"Return Order",
                                 "Return Order No.") THEN BEGIN
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 CLEAR(TableSalesHeaderRetour_G);
                               END;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               CLEAR(TableSalesLineretour_G);
                             END;

                            // Calcul Rpd
                             // <-- 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
                             RPDDate := DateFact_G;
                             GetPostingDateFromSaleRetRecp("Return Receipt Line", RPDDate);
                             // --> 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001

                             IF TableItem_G.GET("Return Receipt Line"."No.") THEN BEGIN
                               // <-- 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
                               Res_PolTaxMtt_G := TableItem_G.CalcUnitPollutionTaxe(RPDDate, "Return Receipt Line"."Location Code",
                               "Return Receipt Line"."Variant Code");
                               //Res_PolTaxMtt_G := TableItem_G.CalcUnitPollutionTaxe(DateFact_G, "Return Receipt Line"."Location Code",
                               //"Return Receipt Line"."Variant Code");
                               // --> 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
                             END;
                             //-->20150402-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL-001
                             // Calcul CVO
                             CLEAR( "CVO Credit Tax Code_P");
                           //  IF TableItem_G."CVO Corn" THEN BEGIN
                            //   "CVO Credit Tax Code_P":='CVO_MAIS';
                             //END;

                             // --> 20150818-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-KRE-003
                             //BEFORE
                             {
                             IF Type = Type::"Charge (Item)"  THEN BEGIN
                                 UnitPriceCVO_G :=0;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               IF TableItem_G."CVO Credit" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                                 "CVO Credit Tax Code_P" :='CVO BLE';
                                 UnitPriceCVO_G := TableSalesLine_G.CalcCVO(TableSalesLine_G."CMC Campaign No.","CVO Credit Tax Code_P",TableItem_G) ;
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 UnitPriceCVO_G :=0;
                               END;
                             END;
                             }

                             //AFTER
                             IF Type = Type::"Charge (Item)"  THEN BEGIN
                                 RetUnitPriceCVO_G :=0;
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                               IF TableItem_G."CVO Credit" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                                 "CVO Credit Tax Code_P" :='CVO BLE';
                                 RetUnitPriceCVO_G :=TableSalesLineretour_G.CalcCVO(TableSalesLineretour_G."CMC Campaign No.",
                                                  "CVO Credit Tax Code_P",TableItem_G);
                               END ELSE BEGIN
                                 RetUnitPriceCVO_G :=0;
                               END;
                             END;
                             //<-- 20150818-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-KRE-003
                             //<-- 20150402-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL-001

                             // Acces Bl Origine t 36 par N› article a letter
                             CLEAR(BlOrigine_G);
                             //-->20150402-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL-001
                             CLEAR(TableSalesInvoiceHeader_G);
                             //<--20150402-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL-001
                             CLEAR(TableSalesInvoiceLine_G);
                             CLEAR(TableSalesHeader_G);
                             CLEAR(TableSalesShipmentLine_G);
                             CLEAR(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G);
                             CLEAR(TableSalesLine_G);
                           //  CLEAR(TableSeason_G);
                             CLEAR(TableSalesInvoiceHeader_G);
                             CLEAR(TableSalesInvoiceLine_G);
                             CLEAR(TableSalesInvoiceHeader_G);
                             CLEAR(WREMAVG_G);
                             CLEAR(WREMREX_G);
                             CLEAR(WREMRFC_G);

                             IF "Return Receipt Line"."Appl.-from Item Entry" <>  0 THEN BEGIN
                               TableSalesShipmentLine_G.SETRANGE( TableSalesShipmentLine_G."Item Shpt. Entry No."
                               ,"Return Receipt Line"."Appl.-from Item Entry");
                               IF TableSalesShipmentLine_G.FINDFIRST() THEN BEGIN
                                 BlOrigine_G := TableSalesShipmentLine_G."Document No.";
                                 TableSalesHeader_G.SETRANGE(TableSalesHeader_G."No.",TableSalesShipmentLine_G."Order No.");
                                 IF NOT TableSalesHeader_G.FINDFIRST() THEN BEGIN
                                   CLEAR(TableSalesHeader_G);
                                 END ELSE BEGIN
                                   IF NOT TableSalesLine_G.GET(TableSalesHeader_G."Document Type",
                                     TableSalesShipmentLine_G."Order No.",TableSalesShipmentLine_G."Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
                                     CLEAR(TableSalesLine_G);
                                   END ELSE BEGIN
                                     CLEAR(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G);
                                     IF TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G.GET(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Document Type"::Order,TableSalesLine_G."Document No.",
                                       TableSalesLine_G."Line No.",'REX')   THEN BEGIN
                                       //WREMREX_G  :='REX';
                                        WREMREX_G:=TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Final Price";
                                     END ELSE BEGIN
                                        WREMREX_G := 0;
                                     END;

                                     CLEAR(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G);
                                     IF TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G.GET(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Document Type"::Order,TableSalesLine_G."Document No.",
                                       TableSalesLine_G."Line No.",'AVG')   THEN BEGIN
                                      // WREMAVG_G  :='AVG';
                                        WREMAVG_G:=TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Final Price";
                                     END ELSE BEGIN
                                       WREMAVG_G := 0;
                                     END;
                                     CLEAR(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G);
                                    // IF TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G.GET(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G."Document Type"::Order,TableSalesLine_G."Document No.",
                                     //  TableSalesLine_G."Line No.",'RFC')   THEN BEGIN
                                     IF TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G1.GET(TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G1."Document Type"::Invoice,
                                     TableSalesLineretour_G."Valued From Doc. No.",
                                       TableSalesLineretour_G."Valued From Doc. Line No.",'RFC')   THEN BEGIN

                                       //  WREMRFC_G  :='RFC';
                                   //  WREMRFC_G:=TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G1."Final Price";
                                      WREMRFC_G:= TableCMCSaleDocDiscount_G1."Discount Amount" ;
                                     END ELSE BEGIN
                                       WREMRFC_G := 0;
                                     END;

                                     //BEFORE

                                     // IF NOT TableSeason_G.GET(TableSalesLine_G."Season ID No.")  THEN BEGIN
                                     //  CLEAR(TableSeason_G);
                                     //END;


                                     //AFTER
                                    {  CLEAR(TableSeason_G);
                                      IF TableSalesLine_G."Item Season Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                        IF NOT TableSeason_G.GET(TableSalesLine_G."Item Season Code")  THEN BEGIN

                                        END;
                                      END;
                                     }

                                     IF TableSalesLine_G."Item Season Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                       IF NOT TabSaison_G.GET ( TableSalesLine_G."Item Season Code") THEN BEGIN
                                       CLEAR(TabSaison_G);
                                       END;
                                     END;


                                     // ACCES T113 pour recuperer N› facture et recuperer date ech sur Facture Enregistree t112
                                     TableSalesInvoiceLine_G.SETRANGE("Shipment No.", TableSalesLine_G."Shipment No.");
                                     //-->20150402-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL-001
                                     //BEFORE
                                     {
                                     IF TableSalesInvoiceLine_G.FINDSET() THEN BEGIN
                                       IF NOT TableSalesInvoiceHeader_G.GET(TableSalesInvoiceLine_G."Document No.") THEN BEGIN
                                       CLEAR(TableSalesInvoiceHeader_G);
                                       }
                                       // AFTER

                                     IF TableSalesInvoiceLine_G.FINDLAST() THEN BEGIN
                                       IF NOT TableSalesInvoiceHeader_G.GET(BlOrigine_G) THEN BEGIN
                                       //<--20150402-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-CDL-001
                                       CLEAR(TableSalesInvoiceHeader_G);  // ajout ESL 20150715
                                       END;
                                     END ELSE BEGIN
                                       CLEAR(TableSalesInvoiceLine_G);
                                     END;

                                     // Ligne Commentaire Plusieurs Possibles
                                     CLEAR(WtextComment_G);
                                     TableSalesCommentLine_G.SETRANGE("Document Type",TableSalesCommentLine_G."Document Type"::Order);
                                     TableSalesCommentLine_G.SETRANGE("No.",TableSalesLine_G."Document No.");
                                     TableSalesCommentLine_G.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",TableSalesLine_G."Line No.");
                                     TableSalesCommentLine_G.SETRANGE(Type,TableSalesCommentLine_G.Type::Commercial);
                                     IF TableSalesCommentLine_G.FINDSET() THEN BEGIN
                                       REPEAT
                                        WtextComment_G := WtextComment_G  + TableSalesCommentLine_G.Comment + ' ' ;
                                       UNTIL TableSalesCommentLine_G.NEXT = 0;
                                     END;
                                   END;
                                 END;
                               END;
                               // <-- 20220725-CL118571-XPE-001
                               GetPlanDelDateFromSaleRetRecp("Return Receipt Line", PlannedDeliveryDate_G);
                               // --> 20220725-CL118571-XPE-001
                               // <-- 20220822-CLA119559-XPE-001
                               //MiniPrice_G := GetMiniPrice("Return Receipt Line"."No.", "Return Receipt Line"."Variant Code");
                               MiniPrice_G := 0;
                               SalesHeaderComment := GetHeaderComments(TableSalesCommentLine_G."Document Type"::Order, TableSalesHeader_G."No.");
                               GetDataFromSalesRetRecep("Return Receipt Line");
                               // --> 20220822-CLA119559-XPE-001

                             END;
                           IF TableSalesOrderGrooups_G.GET("SO Group Code") THEN;
                           END;
                         END;

      }

    PROCEDURE MessageDebug@1000000000(Message_P@1000000000 : Text[250]);
    BEGIN
      IF ((USERID = 'ERIC SAINT-LEGER') OR (USERID = 'CHRISTOPHE DAL')) THEN BEGIN
        IF MessageDebugActif_G THEN BEGIN
          MESSAGE(Message_P);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetPostingDateFromSalesShipt@1000000003(SalesShipmentLine@1000000000 : Record 111;VAR RpdDate@1000000001 : Date);
    VAR
      Salesheader@1000000002 : Record 36;
      SalesLine@1000000003 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      // <-- 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
      IF (Salesheader.GET(Salesheader."Document Type"::Order,
                          SalesShipmentLine."Order No.")) THEN BEGIN
        IF (SalesLine.GET (Salesheader."Document Type",
                           Salesheader."No.",
                           SalesShipmentLine."Order Line No.")) THEN BEGIN
          GetPostingDate(Salesheader, SalesLine, RpdDate);
        END;
      END;
      // --> 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetPostingDateFromSaleRetRecp@1000000004(ReturnReceiptLine@1000000002 : Record 6661;VAR RpdDate@1000000003 : Date);
    VAR
      Salesheader@1000000001 : Record 36;
      SalesLine@1000000000 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      // <-- 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
      IF (Salesheader.GET(Salesheader."Document Type"::"Return Order",
                          ReturnReceiptLine."Return Order No.")) THEN BEGIN
        IF (SalesLine.GET (Salesheader."Document Type",
                           Salesheader."No.",
                           ReturnReceiptLine."Return Order Line No.")) THEN BEGIN
          GetPostingDate(Salesheader, SalesLine, RpdDate);
        END;
      END;
      // --> 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetPostingDate@1000000001(SalesHeader@1000000000 : Record 36;SalesLine@1000000002 : Record 37;VAR RpdDate@1000000001 : Date);
    VAR
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000003 : Record 112;
      NewRPDDate@1000000004 : Date;
    BEGIN
      // <-- 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
      CASE SalesHeader."Document Type" OF
        SalesHeader."Document Type"::Order:
          NewRPDDate:=SalesHeader."Order Date";
        SalesHeader."Document Type"::"Return Order": BEGIN
          NewRPDDate:=SalesHeader."Order Date";
          IF (SalesLine."Valued From Doc. No." <> '' ) AND SalesInvoiceHeader.GET(SalesLine."Valued From Doc. No.") THEN
            NewRPDDate := SalesInvoiceHeader."Document Date";
        END;
        SalesHeader."Document Type"::Invoice:
          NewRPDDate:=SalesHeader."Document Date";
        SalesHeader."Document Type"::"Credit Memo": BEGIN
          NewRPDDate:=SalesHeader."Document Date";
          IF (SalesLine."Valued From Doc. No." <> '' ) AND SalesInvoiceHeader.GET(SalesLine."Valued From Doc. No.") THEN
            NewRPDDate := SalesInvoiceHeader."Document Date";
        END;
      END;
      IF ((NewRPDDate <> 0D) AND (NewRPDDate < RpdDate)) THEN
        RpdDate:= NewRPDDate;
      // --> 20190304-Rpt01-CL59226-XPE-001
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetMiniPrice@1000000008(ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20];VariantCode@1000000002 : Code[20];OrderDate@1000000003 : Date) ReturnValue : Decimal;

    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
